1/2 teaspoon baking powder          in eggs one at a time, beating well after each addition.
3/4 teaspoon baking soda
                                    Sift together flour, baking powder, baking soda and salt.
1/2 teaspoon salt
1/2 teaspoon vanilla extract
                                    Add vanilla to the evaporated milk. Alternately add flour mixture and

                                    Bake at 375 degrees F (190 degrees C) for 45 minutes.
